# SWEEP #10 · TERMINAL 28 — THE ADMIN SERVER ROUTES, PART 1 (positions 1–26) · ids P197001–P197650 (round 1) + P197651–P198000, P212001–P212650 (round 2, 2026-10-12)

Territory: `find app/api/admin -name route.ts | sort | sed -n '1,26p'` — re-derived 2026-10-10, 26 files,
`act-as/go` … `rate-limits`. 650 new checks, written by `scripts/sweep/t28s10/checks.mjs` (re-run it:
`node scripts/sweep/t28s10/checks.mjs --base http://localhost:<port> --live <live.json> --screens <screens.json> --md`),
fed by `live.mjs` (the write flows, fixture on French House, every row removed by its own id) and
`screens.mjs` (15 console screens × desktop dark, desktop light, Samsung A35). Zero sign-ins; no request was
ever made without the admin cookie — "does it refuse without a login" is answered by READING (safe-audit rule).

Planned by measurement: before this pass the thinnest files had 31–34 rows and only 7–8 that had ever DRIVEN
the app (agent-runs 7/31, panels-health 7/34, health 7/44, oplog/ack 7/38, attention 8/34, cancelled-today
8/34), so block E's cross-truth drives go hardest there.

| block | what | rows |
|---|---|---|
| A | every write statement: its answer is looked at, and it is narrowed to named rows | 90 |
| B | every diary line lands on the admin's own log | 43 |
| C | every plain-words refusal names a thing and says whether anything changed | 111 |
| D | every id check is anchored and case-insensitive | 20 |
| E | cross-truth: the number on the wire equals an independent service-role read | 107 |
| F | the odd request on every write handler (empty body, not JSON, a verb not exported) | 54 |
| G | every query parameter, new shapes (a decimal, padded whitespace, an UPPER-CASE uuid) | 95 |
| H | the states the happy path never visits (two tabs, a name clash, a stale expectation) | 22 |
| I | the screens rendered and looked at (45) + the live write flows (51) | 96 |
| J | judgement | 12 |
